/api/repos/:owner/:name/contents?branch=<branch>&path=<path>`\n  → `{contents:{type:'file'|'directory', branch, path, name?, content?, commit?:{id,message,createdAt,author}, entries:[{name,type,path,url}]}}`\n  - 不可见仓库 / 不存在的 branch / 不存在的 path → `404 not_found`（与仓库详情同一防枚举语义）\n  - 目录条目顶层由 `tree_json`（全量 `{path: content}` 快照）按键前缀推导，直接子项含 `url`\n  - 同时断言文件链接 accessible name 与 **href 值**；M4a 若采用不同路由会立即在 M3 套件暴露。\n\n**对 M4a 的期望**：在同一路由上扩展（分支选择器/提交历史/Commits 入口/diff/Web 编辑），不重复造第二套路由。若 M4a 侧有既定路由冲突，按契约优先原则先回 Issue #5 thread 20 裁决，再动 M3 侧。\n\n另请负责人注意 2026-09-29T05:50:26.011048074Z\nThread: 35 (open)\nReply to: comment 35\n\n## 根 Issue #1 裁决：本契约已采纳（含 5 条补充约定，2026-09-29）\n\nblob/tree 路由 + contents API 已裁决采纳为 #6/#7 共享基线（裁决全文：issue #5 评论 #42，经 requirements 原文核实 + advisor 独立审查）。登记按下列修订版生效，**docs 契约文本由 PR #11 写入 architecture.md 后随合入发布**：\n\n1. 页面路由：`/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*path`（文件）、`/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*path`（目录）。\n2. 数据 API：`GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/contents?branch=&path=` → `{contents:{type,branch,path,name?,content?,commit?,entries:[{name,type,path,url}]}}`；不可见仓库与未知 branch/path → 404。\n3. **补充约定（裁决新增，M4a 实施时必须遵守）**：\n   - 编码规则：`:branch` 恒为单 URL 段（`encodeURIComponent(branch)`，分支名可含 `/` 如 `feature/api-v2`）；`*path` 按段编码后以 `/` 连接，禁止原始拼接；`entries[].url` 已完整编码，消费时不得二次编码。\n   - 裸路由语义：`/:owner/:repo` 恒为默认分支根列表；分支切换 = 导航到 `/tree/<branch>[/<path>]`（分支必须在 URL 中，REQ-4-3-1/4-3-2 刷新持久化判据强制）。\n   - **约定 4（归 M4a 实现）**：contents 的 `commit` 字段目标语义为\"最后触碰该 path 的提交\"（REQ-4-1 原文）；M3 现状返回分支头 commit，是已登记近似，M4a 负责更正。\n   - **约定 5（归 M4a 实现）**：存在但无提交的分支返回 `{type:'directory', entries:[]}`（200）；仅未知 branch/path → 404。M3 现状为空分支 404，M4a 负责更正。\n4. diff API 形状由 M4a 登记定义，但**必须以 commitId 为键**（§9.1，M6a/#9 硬依赖）。\n\n指派后请负责人在实施计划中确认消费本契约，并把约定 4/5 的更正纳入范围。\n\n\n\n请处理 Issue #6。\n\n对象：local/run#6\n\n发生以下更新：\n- 新 Issue 需求\n- 负责人已变更。\n\n使用 `braid issue view 6 --comments` 查看当前内容。\n"

Seed 机制（契约）\n\n- `backend/src/seed/index.js` 维护注册表；每个业务模块导出 `seedXxx(db)` 幂等函数（按业务键 upsert）。\n- 基础 PR 提供账户种子（alice-dev、bob-reviewer 等，见 docs/seed-data.md）与应用骨架种子。\n- 业务模块在合并时把自己的 seed 注册进总表（`backend/src/seed/all.js` 加一行 import）；\n  **服务器启动时默认执行全部 seed**（`SEED=0` 关闭），因为平台以 `npm run start` 正常启动即需具备初始数据。\n- seed 必须幂等且**不得覆盖用户已产生的数据**：账户种子用 `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`\n  （因此改过密码的账户重启后仍保留新密码）；业务对象按业务键 upsert 同样不得回退用户改动。\n- 场景隔离：验收测试使用唯一后缀对象（`pw-branch-<suffix>` 等，需求原文即如此要求），不做 per-test 重置。\n\n## 7. 前端共享 UI（契约）\n\n- `AppShell`：**全局搜索框在未登录与登录态都渲染**（REQ-3-1 场景 1 由访客使用顶栏 searchbox \"Search\"；\n  2026-09-29 根 Issue #1 裁决修订）；未登录态导航为 logo + Sign in / Sign up，登录态为 + 新建下拉、\n  Issues/PR 图标链接、头像账户菜单。\n- `AccountMenu`：触发按钮 accessible name 为 \"Account menu\"，菜单含当前用户名、组织入口（\"Your organizations\"）、\n  \"Signed in as <user>\"、\"Your repositories\"、\"Settings\"、\"Sign out\"（按需求文本微调以各模块为准）。\n- 表单组件统一输出：label 关联、字段错误（`field` 对应）、aria-describedby 帮助文本。\n- 图标按钮一律 `aria-label`；分段控件计数徽标不进入 accessible name。\n- 路由：`/`、`/login`、`/register`、`/password-reset`、`/settings/*`、`/:owner/:repo(/*)`、`/orgs/:org(/*)`、`/search`。\n  具体页面在业务模块内实现，路由名以需求文本的 URL 语义为准（无需与 GitHub 完全一致）。\n\n### 7.1 仓库读路径契约（M3 发布，M4a/M4b 消费；2026-09-29 根 Issue #1 评论 #42 裁决）\n\n- 页面路由：\n  - `/:owner/:repo`：恒为**默认分支**的根目录列表（概览与 Code 页共用的入口）。\n  - `/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*path`：文件内容页。\n  - `/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*path`：目录列表页（`path` 为空时为该分支根列表）。\n- 数据端点：`GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/contents?branch=<branch>&path=<path>` →\n  `{ contents: { type: 'file'|'directory', branch, path, name?, content?,\n  commit?: { id, message, createdAt, author }, entries: [{ name, type, path, url }] } }`；\n  不可见仓库、未知 branch/path → `404 not_found`（与仓库详情同一防枚举语义）。\n- 约定（全部消费模块必须遵守）：\n  1. **编码规则**：页面 URL 中 `:branch` 恒为**单个 URL 段**，由 `encodeURIComponent(branch)` 产生\n     （分支名可含 `/`，如 `feature/api-v2`）；`*path` 按段 `encodeURIComponent` 后以 `/` 连接；禁止原始拼接。\n     API 的 `branch`/`path` 是 query 原始字符串，服务端只 trim 首尾斜杠。\n  2. **`entries[].url` 消费规则**：由服务端生成、同源绝对路径且已按规则 1 完整编码；消费者原样使用，\n     不得二次编码或重建。\n  3. **裸路由/默认分支语义**：`/:owner/:repo` 恒为默认分支根列表；分支切换 = 导航到\n     `/tree/<branch>[/<path>]`，**分支必须出现在 URL 中**（REQ-4-3-1/4-3-2 的刷新持久化判据强制此点）。\n  4. **`commit` 字段语义**：目标语义为「最后触碰该 path 的提交」（REQ-4-1 \"most recent commit for that\n     branch and path\"）。M3 已发布的近似为「分支头 commit」，属登记的已知近似；实现更正归 M4a（#6），\n     不阻塞 M3。\n  5. **空分支 200 / 缺失 404**：存在但无提交的分支返回 `{type:'directory', entries:[]}`（200）；\n     仅未知 branch/path → 404。实现更正归 M4a（#6）（空分支在 M4b 建分支能力前不可达，M3 范围不可观测）。\n- diff API 形状留给 M4a 登记，但**以 commitId 为键**（§9.1，M6a/#9 硬依赖）。\n\n## 8. 质量设施（基础 PR 提供）"

required\"、\"Review required by branch protection\"），在字段旁逐项显示，一次提交可同时显示多项错误。\n7. **权限不足的 UI 行为分三档**：a) 控件完全隐藏（多数写操作）；b) 只读视图；c) 服务端拒绝。\n   每条需求明确指明档次，实现时以需求原文为准。\n\n## 4. 关键用户路径（跨模块主线）\n\n1. 访客 → 验收方案\n\n## 分层\n\n1. **模块自检（各子 Issue 完成条件）**\n   - 后端：Vitest 覆盖权限矩阵、校验规则（用户名/邮箱/密码/分支名/文件路径/标题长度）、seed 幂等。\n   - 前端 + 集成：Playwright 用例逐条对应需求场景 GIVEN/WHEN/THEN；断言用 `getByRole(role, { name })`\n     精确匹配；覆盖成功路径 + 每条需求列出的失败分支（字段错误文案逐字断言）。\n   - 持久化断言：刷新/重开后状态保持（需求反复出现）。\n2. **整合验收（develop → main 整合 PR，根负责人执行）**\n   - 全量 `pnpm e2e` + 平台启动路径冒烟：frontend `npm install && npm run build`，\n     backend `npm install && HOST=0.0.0.0 PORT=3000 npm run start`，120 秒内可访问。\n   - Node 版本核对：`/usr/local/bin/node --version`（20.19.3）存在且一致时以平台入口为准执行检查。\n\n## 场景隔离与数据\n\n- 每条用例以\"fresh unauthenticated browser session\"开始（新 context）。\n- 写操作用 `pw-<suffix>` 唯一后缀对象；seed 幂等，不做 per-test 重置。\n- 失败时保留 trace、console、请求错误；先区分实现/检查/环境问题，不为迎合实现改判据。\n\n## 精确名称断言抽查（高风险点）\n\n- \"Account menu\"、\"Sign out\"、\"Confirm sign out\"、\"Cancel\"（登出对话框）\n- \"Username already exists\"、\"Verification code is invalid\"、\"Password updated\"\n- \"Create branch: <name>\"、\"Invalid branch\"、\"Branch <name>\"（选择器按钮）\n- \"Add comment\"、\"Add single comment\"、\"Start a review\"、\"Review changes\"、\"Submit review\"\n- \"Merge pull request\"、\"Confirm merge\"、\"Review required by branch protection\"\n- \"Search issues\"、\"Search assignees\"、\"Find branch\"、\"Find a repository\"\n- 选择器 option 的 role=option 与精确 username/label 名称"

具体页面在业务模块内实现，路由名以需求文本的 URL 语义为准（无需与 GitHub 完全一致）。\n\n### 7.1 仓库读路径契约（M3 发布，M4a/M4b 消费；2026-09-29 根 Issue #1 评论 #42 裁决）\n\n- 页面路由：\n  - `/:owner/:repo`：恒为**默认分支**的根目录列表（概览与 Code 页共用的入口）。\n  - `/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*path`：文件内容页。\n  - `/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*path`：目录列表页（`path` 为空时为该分支根列表）。\n- 数据端点：`GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/contents?branch=<branch>&path=<path>` →\n  `{ contents: { type: 'file'|'directory', branch, path, name?, content?,\n  commit?: { id, message, createdAt, author }, entries: [{ name, type, path, url }] } }`；\n  不可见仓库、未知 branch/path → `404 not_found`（与仓库详情同一防枚举语义）。\n- 约定（全部消费模块必须遵守）：\n  1. **编码规则**：页面 URL 中 `:branch` 恒为**单个 URL 段**，由 `encodeURIComponent(branch)` 产生\n     （分支名可含 `/`，如 `feature/api-v2`）；`*path` 按段 `encodeURIComponent` 后以 `/` 连接；禁止原始拼接。\n     API 的 `branch`/`path` 是 query 原始字符串，服务端只 trim 首尾斜杠。\n  2. **`entries[].url` 消费规则**：由服务端生成、同源绝对路径且已按规则 1 完整编码；消费者原样使用，\n     不得二次编码或重建。\n  3. **裸路由/默认分支语义**：`/:owner/:repo` 恒为默认分支根列表；分支切换 = 导航到\n     `/tree/<branch>[/<path>]`，**分支必须出现在 URL 中**（REQ-4-3-1/4-3-2 的刷新持久化判据强制此点）。\n  4. **`commit` 字段语义**：目标语义为「最后触碰该 path 的提交」（REQ-4-1 \"most recent commit for that\n     branch and path\"）。M3 已发布的近似为「分支头 commit」，属登记的已知近似；实现更正归 M4a（#6），\n     不阻塞 M3。\n  5. **空分支 200 / 缺失 404**：存在但无提交的分支返回 `{type:'directory', entries:[]}`（200）；\n     仅未知 branch/path → 404。实现更正归 M4a（#6）（空分支在 M4b 建分支能力前不可达，M3 范围不可观测）。\n- diff API 形状留给 M4a 登记，但**以 commitId 为键**（§9.1，M6a/#9 硬依赖）。\n\n## 8. 质量设施（基础 PR 提供）\n\n- Vitest：后端单测（权限、校验规则）；前端组件测试按需。\n- Playwright：`e2e/` 目录，每模块一个 spec 文件；验收用例直接对应需求场景（GIVEN/WHEN/THEN）。\n  断言使用 getByRole + 精确 name。\n- 脚本：`pnpm dev`（portless 并行起前后端）、`pnpm test`、`pnpm e2e`、`pnpm build`。\n\n## 9. 修订记录（advisor 咨询后确定，2026-09-29）\n\n1. **commit 共享不可变**：commit 为全局唯一不可变对象（不按仓库复制）；branch 仅为指针；\n   fork = 在 fork 仓库中创建指向既有 commit 的分支指针；fork 后新 commit 归属 fork 仓库且不影响源仓库。\n   理由：stale review（按 compare commit）、Checks、diff 全部自然落在 commitId 键上；fork 无复制开销。\n2. **合并语义（REQ-6 消费）**：merge base = compare 分支与 base 分支的共同祖先 commit。\n   逐文件三方合并：仅 compare 侧变更 → 取 compare；仅 base 侧变更 → 保持 base；\n   双侧都改且内容不同 → 冲突：该 PR 判定 unmergeable，合并区显示冲突说明且按钮禁用。\n   merge commit 的 tree 在事务内计算并作为新 commit 落库（parents = base head + compare head）。\n3. **前端权限镜像**：后端在仓库/组织作用域的响应中返回 `viewerPermission`（仓库角色或 org 角色枚举）；\n   前端只读取该字段决定控件渲染（隐藏 vs 只读 vs 可操作），不自行计算权限。\n   验收同时断言控件缺席（按需求指明的档次）与服务端拒绝。\n4. **文案契约**：基础 PR 交付 `frontend/src/contracts/copy.ts`（或等价位置），把需求中被断言的\n   accessible name 与错误消息定义为常量；组件与 Playwright 测试都引用它，禁止散落字面量。\n5. **Checks 键**：check 状态按 `(compareCommitId, checkName)` 存储与展示；compare 分支新 commit 后\n   新 compare commit 的必需检查初始为 pending（与 review stale 对称）。\n6. **测试隔离强化**：账户可跨模块共享；业务实体（组织/仓库/issue/PR 等）模块自有 + 唯一 token 命名；\n   列表/搜索/计数断言必须用唯一 token 限定范围，禁止全局计数或排序断言。seed 一律按业务键 upsert、顺序无关。\n7. **依赖库评估记录（better-auth，时间盒结论）**：本产品需要固定验证码找回、字段级多项错误、\n   精确错误文案、仓库级 5 角色授权、团队层级不传播权限、改密/登出即时失效会话。\n   better-auth email/password 与 organization 插件均以自身默认流与角色模型为中心，映射以上需求需大量\n   对抗性定制（覆盖默认错误文案与流程），自建（表结构见 §5）更直接、可控，选定自建。\n8. **better-sqlite3 安装风险**：基础 PR 第一项工作为在平台 npm 环境（PATH=/usr/local/bin:$PATH）做干净\n   `npm install` 实测；失败保留首轮日志并改用有 prebuild 的锁定版本或替代驱动，结论记录在 PR。\n9. **拆分调整**：M4 拆为 M4a（内容模型+浏览+历史+diff）、M4b（分支+代码搜索+Web 编辑）；\n   M6 拆为 M6a（保护规则+Checks+PR 生命周期+三视图）、M6b（行内评论+评审+stale+合并门控）。\n   M6a 硬依赖 M4a 的 diff API；M6b 依赖 M6a。\n10. **基础 PR 提出的 6 项契约增量（根 Issue #1 评论 #8 全部采纳，2026-09-29）**：\n    1. `users.email_verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1`：承载 REQ-1-1-1 要求的“已验证邮箱状态”。\n    2. `commit_parents(commit_id, parent_commit_id, position)`：§9.2 的合并 commit 有两个父提交；\n       `commits.parent_id` 保留为第一父。\n    3. `issue_milestones(issue_id PRIMARY KEY, milestone_id)`：一个 issue 至多一个里程碑。\n    4. seed **随启动默认执行**（`SEED=0` 关闭），取代“非生产或显式 SEED=1”；理由与幂等约束见 §6。\n    5. 会话 cookie 不带 `Secure`（HTTP 交付）；理由见 §3。\n    6. 字段错误存在时**始终**返回 `errors[]`（含单条），`error` 为首项；理由见 §3。\n    实现证据：PR 改密保留调用者当前会话、仅吊销该用户其它会话；找回密码吊销该用户全部会话且不自动登录；\n    找回密码不泄露账户存在性（`forgot-password` 对已注册/未注册邮箱响应逐字节一致，`reset-password` 对未知邮箱与\n    错误验证码都返回字段 `code` 的 `Verification code is invalid`，且错误码提交时仍一次返回全部字段错误，\n         "type": "text",
        "text": "/**\n * Repository module service (REQ-3).\n *\n * Single implementation of the repository rules used by the HTTP router and by\n * the Vitest suite:\n *  - a repository belongs to exactly one namespace: a personal namespace (a user\n *    name) or an organisation namespace (an organisation name);\n *  - creating a repository needs permission in the target namespace: a personal\n *    namespace only accepts its own user, an organisation namespace only its\n *    Owners (docs/architecture.md §4 — a plain Member has no creation rights);\n *  - initialisation (default branch + README + initial commit) happens in the\n *    same transaction as the repository row, so a failure leaves nothing behind;\n *  - a fork copies branch *pointers* to the shared immutable commits\n *    (docs/architecture.md §9.1); it never copies commit rows and never writes\n *    back to the source repository;\n *  - a private source repository can only be forked as private (enforced here,\n *    not in the browser);\n *  - the browser never computes permissions: responses carry `viewerPermission`.\n */\nimport { transact } from '../../db/index.js';\nimport { canViewRepo, orgRole, viewerPermissionForRepo } from '../../permissions/index.js';\nimport { messages } from '../../contracts/messages.js';\nimport { forbidden, fieldError, validationError } from '../../http/errors.js';\n\nexport const DEFAULT_BRANCH_NAME = 'main';\nexport const README_PATH = 'README.md';\nexport const INITIAL_COMMIT_MESSAGE = 'Initial commit';\n\n/** Repository names: letters/digits/`.`/`_`/`-`, no leading dot or hyphen. */\nconst REPO_NAME_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]*$/;\n\nfunction text(raw) {\n  return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.trim() : '';\n}\n\nfunction isTruthy(raw) {\n  return raw === true || raw === 'true' || raw === 'on' || raw === 1 || raw === '1';\n}\n\nfunction pick(result) {\n  return { code: result.code, message: result.message, field: result.field };\n}\n\n/** @returns {{ok:true, value:string, field:string}|{ok:false, code:string, message:string, field:string}} */\nexport function validateRepoName(raw, field = 'name') {\n  const value = text(raw);\n  if (!value) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'repo_name_required', message: messages.REPO_NAME_REQUIRED, field };\n  }\n  const invalid = value.length > 100 || !REPO_NAME_RE.test(value) || value.includes('..') || value.endsWith('.');\n  if (invalid) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'repo_name_invalid', message: messages.REPO_NAME_INVALID, field };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\nexport function validateVisibility(raw, field = 'visibility') {\n  const value = text(raw);\n  if (value !== 'public' && value !== 'private') {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'visibility_invalid', message: messages.VISIBILITY_INVALID, field };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve an owner identifier to a namespace.\n * @returns {{kind:'user', name:string, userId:number}|{kind:'org', name:string, orgId:number}|null}\n */\nexport function resolveNamespace(db, ownerName) {\n  const name = text(ownerName);\n  if (!name) return null;\n  const user = db.prepare('SELECT id, username FROM users WHERE username = ?').get(name);\n  if (user) return { kind: 'user', name: user.username, userId: user.id };\n  const org = db.prepare('SELECT id, name FROM organizations WHERE name = ?').get(name);\n  if (org) return { kind: 'org', name: org.name, orgId: org.id };\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** Can `user` create a repository in this namespace? */\nexport function canCreateInNamespace(db, user, namespace) {\n  if (!user || !namespace) return false;\n  if (namespace.kind === 'user') return Number(namespace.userId) === Number(user.id);\n  return orgRole(db, user, namespace.orgId) === 'owner';\n}\n\nexport function getRepoById(db, id) {\n  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM repos WHERE id = ?').get(id) ?? null;\n}\n\n/** Repository lookup by owner identifier + name (case-insensitive name). */\nexport function findRepoByOwnerAndName(db, ownerName, repoName) {\n  const namespace = resolveNamespace(db, ownerName);\n  if (!namespace) return null;\n  return findRepoInNamespace(db, namespace, text(repoName));\n}\n\nexport function findRepoInNamespace(db, namespace, repoName) {\n  if (!namespace || !repoName) return null;\n  if (namespace.kind === 'user') {\n    return (\n      db\n        .prepare('SELECT * FROM repos WHERE owner_user_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE')\n        .get(namespace.userId, repoName) ?? null\n    );\n  }\n  return (\n    db\n      .prepare('SELECT * FROM repos WHERE owner_org_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE')\n      .get(namespace.orgId, repoName) ?? null\n  );\n}\n\n/** Namespace identifier of a repository (\"owner\" in every route). */\nexport function ownerLabel(db, repo) {\n  if (!repo) return null;\n  if (repo.owner_user_id != null) {\n    const row = db.prepare('SELECT username FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(repo.owner_user_id);\n    return row?.username ?? null;\n  }\n  const row = db.prepare('SELECT name FROM organizations WHERE id = ?').get(repo.owner_org_id);\n  return row?.name ?? null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Update time of a repository: the newest commit it owns, else its creation time\n * (Issue #5 comment #20 §2 — no extra column in the shared schema).\n */\nexport function repoUpdatedAt(db, repo) {\n  const row = db.prepare('SELECT MAX(created_at) AS updated_at FROM commits WHERE repo_id = ?').get(repo.id);\n  return row?.updated_at || repo.created_at;\n}\n\nexport function findDefaultBranch(db, repo) {\n  if (!repo.default_branch_id) return null;\n  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM branches WHERE id = ?').get(repo.default_branch_id) ?? null;\n}\n\nconst entryUrl = (owner, repoName, branch, path, type) =>\n  `/${owner}/${repoName}/${type === 'directory' ? 'tree' : 'blob'}/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}/${path\n    .split('/')\n    .map(encodeURIComponent)\n    .join('/')}`;\n\nfunction groupEntries(tree, prefix) {\n  const entries = new Map();\n  for (const key of Object.keys(tree)) {\n    if (!key.startsWith(prefix)) continue;\n    const segments = key.slice(prefix.length).split('/');\n    const name = segments[0];\n    const isFile = segments.length === 1;\n    const previous = entries.get(name);\n    if (!previous || (previous.type === 'file' && !isFile)) {\n      entries.set(name, { name, type: isFile ? 'file' : 'directory' });\n    }\n  }\n  return [...entries.values()].sort((a, b) => {\n    if (a.type !== b.type) return a.type === 'directory' ? -1 : 1;\n    return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Top-level entries of the default branch tree. `tree_json` is a full\n * `{path: content}` snapshot (docs/architecture.md §5), so directories are the\n * prefixes of nested paths.\n */\nexport function treeEntries(db, repo) {\n  const branch = findDefaultBranch(db, repo);\n  if (!branch?.head_commit_id) return [];\n  const commit = db.prepare('SELECT tree_json FROM commits WHERE id = ?').get(branch.head_commit_id);\n  const owner = ownerLabel(db, repo);\n  return groupEntries(readTree(commit?.tree_json), '').map((entry) => ({\n    ...entry,\n    path: entry.name,\n    // GitHub-style destination; the content pages are read-only pages of REQ-4.\n    url: entryUrl(owner, repo.name, branch.name, entry.name, entry.type),\n  }));\n}\n\nfunction readTree(treeJson) {\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(treeJson || '{}');\n  } catch {\n    return {};\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Read one branch/path of a repository (REQ-3-3 click-through, REQ-4-1 read\n * path). Returns `null` when the branch or path does not exist.\n */\nexport function repoContents(db, repo, branchName, rawPath) {\n  const branch = db\n    .prepare('SELECT * FROM branches WHERE repo_id = ? AND name = ?')\n    .get(repo.id, text(branchName));\n  if (!branch?.head_commit_id) return null;\n  const commit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM commits WHERE id = ?').get(branch.head_commit_id);\n  const tree = readTree(commit?.tree_json);\n  const path = text(rawPath).replace(/^\\/+|\\/+$/g, '');\n  const owner = ownerLabel(db, repo);\n  const describe = (type, entryPath, entries) => ({\n    type,\n    branch: branch.name,\n    path: entryPath,\n    entries: (entries ?? []).map((entry) => ({\n      ...entry,\n      path: entryPath ? `${entryPath}/${entry.name}` : entry.name,\n      url: entryUrl(owner, repo.name, branch.name, entryPath ? `${entryPath}/${entry.name}` : entry.name, entry.type),\n    })),\n  });\n\n  if (!path) return describe('directory', '', groupEntries(tree, ''));\n  if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(tree, path)) {\n    const author = db.prepare('SELECT username FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(commit.author_id);\n    return {\n      type: 'file',\n      branch: branch.name,\n      path,\n      name: path.split('/').pop(),\n      content: tree[path],\n      commit: {\n        id: commit.id,\n        message: commit.message,\n        createdAt: commit.created_at,\n        author: author?.username ?? null,\n      },\n      entries: [],\n    };\n  }\n  const children = groupEntries(tree, `${path}/`);\n  if (children.length) return describe('directory', path, children);\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport function serializeRepoSummary(db, repo) {\n  const owner = repo.owner ?? ownerLabel(db, repo);\n  return {\n    owner,\n    name: repo.name,\n    description: repo.description,\n    visibility: repo.visibility,\n    updatedAt: repoUpdatedAt(db, repo),\n    path: `/${owner}/${repo.name}`,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function serializeRepoDetail(db, user, repo) {\n  const owner = ownerLabel(db, repo);\n  const branch = findDefaultBranch(db, repo);\n  let forkSource = null;\n  if (repo.fork_source_id) {\n    const source = getRepoById(db, repo.fork_source_id);\n    if (source) forkSource = { owner: ownerLabel(db, source), name: source.name };\n  }\n  return {\n    owner,\n    name: repo.name,\n    description: repo.description,\n    visibility: repo.visibility,\n    defaultBranch: branch?.name ?? null,\n    forkSource,\n    files: treeEntries(db, repo),\n    updatedAt: repoUpdatedAt(db, repo),\n    viewerPermission: viewerPermissionForRepo(db, user, repo),\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Global repository search (REQ-3-1). Results are filtered by the *same* access\n * rule as every other view, so an unauthorised private repository neither appears\n * in the list nor leaks its existence.\n */\nexport function searchRepositories(db, user, rawQuery) {\n  const query = text(rawQuery);\n  if (!query) return [];\n  const like = `%${query.replace(/[\\\\%_]/g, (character) => `\\\\${character}`)}%`;\n  const rows = db\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT r.*, COALESCE(u.username, o.name) AS owner\n         FROM repos r\n         LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = r.owner_user_id\n         LEFT JOIN organizations o ON o.id = r.owner_org_id\n        WHERE r.name LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\\\'\n           OR COALESCE(u.username, o.name) LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\\\'\n        ORDER BY r.name COLLATE NOCASE`,\n    )\n    .all(like, like);\n  return rows.filter((repo) => canViewRepo(db, user, repo)).map((repo) => serializeRepoSummary(db, repo));\n}\n\n/**\n * Visible repositories of one namespace (owner) — the personal/organisation\n * repository list entry point of REQ-3. Unknown namespaces yield an empty list and\n * `owner: null`; a private repository of the namespace is only listed for viewers\n * that satisfy the shared access rule.\n */\nexport function listNamespaceRepositories(db, user, ownerName) {\n  const namespace = resolveNamespace(db, ownerName);\n  if (!namespace) return { owner: null, repositories: [] };\n  const column = namespace.kind === 'user' ? 'owner_user_id' : 'owner_org_id';\n  const id = namespace.kind === 'user' ? namespace.userId : namespace.orgId;\n  const rows = db\n    .prepare(`SELECT r.*, ? AS owner FROM repos r WHERE r.${column} = ? ORDER BY r.name COLLATE NOCASE`)\n    .all(namespace.name, id);\n  return {\n    owner: { name: namespace.name, kind: namespace.kind },\n    repositories: rows.filter((repo) => canViewRepo(db, user, repo)).map((repo) => serializeRepoSummary(db, repo)),\n  };\n}\n\n/** Namespaces the signed-in user may create a repository in (REQ-3-2-1). */\nexport function namespacesForUser(db, user) {\n  if (!user) return [];\n  const namespaces = [];\n  const personal = db.prepare('SELECT username FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(user.id);\n  if (personal) namespaces.push({ name: personal.username, kind: 'user' });\n  const orgs = db\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT o.name AS name\n         FROM organizations o\n         JOIN org_members m ON m.org_id = o.id AND m.role = 'owner'\n        WHERE m.user_id = ?\n        ORDER BY o.name`,\n    )\n    .all(user.id);\n  for (const org of orgs) namespaces.push({ name: org.name, kind: 'org' });\n  return namespaces;\n}\n\nfunction insertRepoRow(db, { namespace, name, description, visibility, forkSourceId }) {\n  return db\n    .prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO repos (owner_user_id, owner_org_id, name, description, visibility, fork_source_id)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n       RETURNING *`,\n    )\n    .get(\n      namespace.kind === 'user' ? namespace.userId : null,\n      namespace.kind === 'org' ? namespace.orgId : null,\n      name,\n      description,\n      visibility,\n      forkSourceId,\n    );\n}\n\n/** Default branch + README + initial commit, inside the caller's transaction. */\nfunction initializeRepository(db, repo, user, repoName) {\n  const tree = JSON.stringify({ [README_PATH]: `# ${repoName}\\n` });\n  const commit = db\n    .prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO commits (repo_id, parent_id, author_id, message, tree_json)\n       VALUES (?, NULL, ?, ?, ?)\n       RETURNING *`,\n    )\n    .get(repo.id, user.id, INITIAL_COMMIT_MESSAGE, tree);\n  db.prepare(\n    `INSERT INTO commit_files (commit_id, path, status, additions, deletions) VALUES (?, ?, 'added', 1, 0)`,\n  ).run(commit.id, README_PATH);\n  const branch = db\n    .prepare('INSERT INTO branches (repo_id, name, head_commit_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?) RETURNING *')\n    .get(repo.id, DEFAULT_BRANCH_NAME, commit.id);\n  db.prepare('UPDATE repos SET default_branch_id = ? WHERE id = ?').run(branch.id, repo.id);\n}\n\n/**\n * Create a repository (REQ-3-2-1). Validation problems are reported together as\n * field errors; the repository row and its optional initialisation are written in\n * one transaction.\n */\nexport function createRepository(db, user, input = {}) {\n  const name = validateRepoName(input.name);\n  const visibility = validateVisibility(input.visibility);\n  const errors = [];\n  if (!name.ok) errors.push(pick(name));\n  if (!visibility.ok) errors.push(pick(visibility));\n\n  const ownerName = text(input.owner);\n  let namespace = null;\n  if (!ownerName) {\n    errors.push(fieldError('owner_required', messages.OWNER_REQUIRED, 'owner'));\n  } else {\n    namespace = resolveNamespace(db, ownerName);\n    if (!namespace) errors.push(fieldError('owner_not_found', messages.OWNER_NOT_FOUND, 'owner'));\n  }\n  if (errors.length) throw validationError(errors);\n\n  if (!canCreateInNamespace(db, user, namespace)) throw forbidden();\n  if (findRepoInNamespace(db, namespace, name.value)) {\n    throw validationError([fieldError('repo_name_exists', messages.REPO_NAME_EXISTS, 'name')]);\n  }\n\n  const description = typeof input.description === 'string' ? input.description.trim() : '';\n  const initReadme = isTruthy(input.initReadme ?? input.init_readme ?? input.addReadme);\n\n  return transact(db, () => {\n    const repo = insertRepoRow(db, {\n      namespace,\n      name: name.value,\n      description,\n      visibility: visibility.value,\n      forkSourceId: null,\n    });\n    if (initReadme) initializeRepository(db, repo, user, repo.name);\n    return getRepoById(db, repo.id);\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Fork a readable source repository into `input.owner` (REQ-3-2-2).\n * The source access check happens in the router (missing/invisible -> 404); here\n * the target namespace permission and the fork rules are enforced.\n */\nexport function forkRepository(db, user, source, input = {}) {\n  const requestedName = text(input.name);\n  const name = validateRepoName(requestedName || source.name);\n  const defaultVisibility = source.visibility === 'private' ? 'private' : 'public';\n  const visibility = validateVisibility(input.visibility ?? defaultVisibility);\n  const errors = [];\n  if (!name.ok) errors.push(pick(name));\n  if (!visibility.ok) errors.push(pick(visibility));\n\n  const ownerName = text(input.owner);\n  let namespace = null;\n  if (!ownerName) {\n    errors.push(fieldError('owner_required', messages.OWNER_REQUIRED, 'owner'));\n  } else {\n    namespace = resolveNamespace(db, ownerName);\n    if (!namespace) errors.push(fieldError('owner_not_found', messages.OWNER_NOT_FOUND, 'owner'));\n  }\n  if (errors.length) throw validationError(errors);\n\n  if (!canCreateInNamespace(db, user, namespace)) throw forbidden();\n  if (findRepoInNamespace(db, namespace, name.value)) {\n    throw validationError([fieldError('repo_name_exists', messages.REPO_NAME_EXISTS, 'name')]);\n  }\n\n  // A private source can only be forked as private; the server decides.\n  const forkVisibility = source.visibility === 'private' ? 'private' : visibility.value;\n\n  return transact(db, () => {\n    const fork = insertRepoRow(db, {\n      namespace,\n      name: name.value,\n      description: source.description,\n      visibility: forkVisibility,\n      forkSourceId: source.id,\n    });\n    // Copy branch pointers only: commits are shared, immutable objects (§9.1).\n    let defaultBranchId = null;\n    const branches = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM branches WHERE repo_id = ? ORDER BY id').all(source.id);\n    for (const branch of branches) {\n      const copy = db\n        .prepare('INSERT INTO branches (repo_id, name, head_commit_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?) RETURNING *')\n        .get(fork.id, branch.name, branch.head_commit_id);\n      if (branch.id === source.default_branch_id) defaultBranchId = copy.id;\n    }\n    db.prepare('UPDATE repos SET default_branch_id = ? WHERE id = ?').run(defaultBranchId, fork.id);\n    return getRepoById(db, fork.id);\n  });\n}\n\n/** Change repository visibility (REQ-3-4). The router enforces admin+. */\nexport function updateVisibility(db, repo, rawVisibility) {\n  const visibility = validateVisibility(rawVisibility);\n  if (!visibility.ok) throw validationError([pick(visibility)]);\n  transact(db, () => {\n    db.prepare('UPDATE repos SET visibility = ? WHERE id = ?').run(visibility.value, repo.id);\n  });\n  return getRepoById(db, repo.id);\n}\n"

'Repository created by Playwright',\n        visibility: 'private',\n        initReadme: true,\n=== permissions ===\n/**\n * Shared permission model (docs/architecture.md §4 — single implementation).\n *\n * Roles are NOT an automatic cumulative ladder: an operation states the role it\n * needs and `roleAtLeast()` compares them in the documented order\n * read < triage < write < maintain < admin. An organisation Owner is an Admin\n * for every repository of that organisation.\n *\n * A user's effective repository role is the highest of\n *   - `admin` when the user owns the repository personally,\n *   - `admin` when the user is an Owner of the owning organisation,\n *   - direct grants on the repository,\n *   - grants to teams the user is a *direct* member of.\n * Team hierarchy never propagates membership or permissions.\n */\n\nimport { forbidden } from '../http/errors.js';\n\nexport const ROLE_ORDER = ['read', 'triage', 'write', 'maintain', 'admin'];\n\nconst ROLE_RANK = new Map(ROLE_ORDER.map((role, index) => [role, index]));\n\n/** @param {string|null|undefined} role */\nexport function isRepoRole(role) {\n  return typeof role === 'string' && ROLE_RANK.has(role);\n}\n\n/** Highest of two roles (null-safe). */\nexport function maxRole(a, b) {\n  const rankA = isRepoRole(a) ? ROLE_RANK.get(a) : -1;\n  const rankB = isRepoRole(b) ? ROLE_RANK.get(b) : -1;\n  if (rankA < 0 && rankB < 0) return null;\n  return rankA >= rankB ? a : b;\n}\n\n/** Does `role` satisfy `required`? Null role never satisfies anything. */\nexport function roleAtLeast(role, required) {\n  if (!isRepoRole(role) || !isRepoRole(required)) return false;\n  return ROLE_RANK.get(role) >= ROLE_RANK.get(required);\n}\n\n/**\n * Effective role of a user on a repository.\n *\n * @param {import('better-sqlite3').Database} db\n * @param {{id:number}|null} user  public or raw user row (null = anonymous)\n * @param {{id:number, owner_user_id:number|null, owner_org_id:number|null}} repo\n * @returns {'read'|'triage'|'write'|'maintain'|'admin'|null}\n */\nexport function effectiveRepoRole(db, user, repo) {\n  if (!user || !repo) return null;\n  if (repo.owner_user_id != null && Number(repo.owner_user_id) === Number(user.id)) return 'admin';\n\n  let role = null;\n\n  if (repo.owner_org_id != null) {\n    const membership = db\n      .prepare('SELECT role FROM org_members WHERE org_id = ? AND user_id = ?')\n      .get(repo.owner_org_id, user.id);\n    if (membership?.role === 'owner') return 'admin';\n  }\n\n  const direct = db\n    .prepare('SELECT role FROM repo_grants WHERE repo_id = ? AND subject_user_id = ?')\n    .get(repo.id, user.id);\n  if (direct) role = maxRole(role, direct.role);\n\n  // Direct team memberships only — hierarchy does not propagate permissions.\n  const teamGrants = db\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT g.role AS role\n         FROM repo_grants g\n         JOIN team_members tm ON tm.team_id = g.subject_team_id\n        WHERE g.repo_id = ? AND tm.user_id = ?`,\n    )\n    .all(repo.id, user.id);\n  for (const grant of teamGrants) role = maxRole(role, grant.role);\n\n  return role;\n}\n\n/**\n * Role of a user inside an organisation ('owner' | 'member' | null).\n */\nexport function orgRole(db, user, orgId) {\n  if (!user || orgId == null) return null;\n  const membership = db\n    .prepare('SELECT role FROM org_members WHERE org_id = ? AND user_id = ?')\n    .get(orgId, user.id);\n  return membership?.role ?? null;\n}\n\n/** Can the user see the repository at all (public repositories are readable)? */\nexport function canViewRepo(db, user, repo) {\n  if (!repo) return false;\n  if (repo.visibility === 'public') return true;\n  return effectiveRepoRole(db, user, repo) != null;\n}\n\n/**\n * The `viewerPermission` value attached to repository/organisation responses\n * (docs/architecture.md §9.3). The frontend only reads this field; it never\n * computes permissions itself.\n *\n * @returns {{ repository?: string|null, organization?: string|null, canView: boolean }}\n */\nexport function viewerPermissionForRepo(db, user, repo) {\n  return {\n    repository: repo ? effectiveRepoRole(db, user, repo) : null,\n    organization: repo?.owner_org_id != null ? orgRole(db, user, repo.owner_org_id) : null,\n    canView: canViewRepo(db, user, repo),\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Express middleware factory for repository-scoped routes. The route must have\n * loaded the repository onto `req.repo`.\n * @param {'read'|'triage'|'write'|'maintain'|'admin'} required\n */\nexport function requireRepoRole(db, required) {\n  return (req, res, next) => {\n    const repo = req.repo;\n    if (!repo) {\n      next(new Error('requireRepoRole() needs req.repo to be loaded first'));\n      return;\n    }\n    const role = effectiveRepoRole(db, req.user, repo);\n    if (!roleAtLeast(role, required)) {\n      next(forbidden());\n      return;\n    }\n    req.repoRole = role;\n    next();\n  };\n}\n=== errors ===\n/**\n * Uniform API error contract (docs/architecture.md §3).\n *\n *   { \"error\":  { \"code\": string, \"message\": string, \"field\"?: string },\n *     \"errors\": [ { code, message, field? }, ... ] }   // present when there are field errors\n *\n * `field` is the form field name so the frontend can render the message next to\n * the matching input. When one submission produces several problems, every\n * problem is reported at once (`errors[]`), never just the first one.\n */\n\nexport class ApiError extends Error {\n  /**\n   * @param {number} status HTTP status\n   * @param {string} code stable machine code, e.g. 'invalid_credentials'\n   * @param {string} message exact user-visible English message\n   * @param {{field?: string, errors?: Array<{code:string,message:string,field?:string}>}} [extra]\n   */\n  constructor(status, code, message, extra = {}) {\n    super(message);\n    this.name = 'ApiError';\n    this.status = status;\n    this.code = code;\n    this.field = extra.field;\n    this.errors = extra.errors;\n  }\n\n  toBody() {\n    const primary = { code: this.code, message: this.message };\n    if (this.field) primary.field = this.field;\n    const body = { error: primary };\n    // `errors[]` lists every field problem of one submission (docs/architecture.md §3).\n    // It is emitted whenever field problems exist so clients can always read the\n    // complete list instead of switching between `error` and `errors`.\n    if (this.errors && this.errors.length) body.errors = this.errors;\n    return body;\n  }\n}\n\n/** One field-level problem. */\nexport function fieldError(code, message, field) {\n  return { code, message, field };\n}\n\n/**\n * Build an ApiError from a list of field problems, so several invalid fields are\n * reported together in a single 422 response.\n * @param {Array<{code:string,message:string,field?:string}>} errors\n */\nexport function validationError(errors) {\n  if (!errors.length) throw new Error('validationError() needs at least one error');\n  const [first] = errors;\n  return new ApiError(422, first.code, first.message, {\n    field: first.field,\n    errors,\n  });\n}\n\n/** 401 helper used by requireAuth and by the auth module. */\nexport function unauthorized(message = 'Authentication required') {\n  return new ApiError(401, 'unauthenticated', message);\n}\n\n/** 403 helper: the caller is known but not allowed to perform the operation. */\nexport function forbidden(message = 'You do not have permission to perform this action') {\n  return new ApiError(403, 'forbidden', message);\n}\n\n/** 404 helper. */\nexport function notFound(message = 'Not found') {\n  return new ApiError(404, 'not_found', message);\n}\n\n/**\n * Express error middleware. Must be registered last.\n * Unknown errors become a generic 500 without leaking internals.\n */\nexport function errorHandler() {\n  // eslint-disable-next-line no-unused-vars -- Express needs the 4-argument shape\n  return (err, req, res, next) => {\n    if (err instanceof ApiError) {\n      res.status(err.status).json(err.toBody());\n      return;\n    }\n    if (err && err.type === 'entity.parse.failed') {\n      res.status(400).json({\n        error: { code: 'invalid_json', message: 'Request body must be valid JSON' },\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n    req.log?.error?.(err);\n    console.error('[api] unhandled error:', err);\n    res.status(500).json({\n      error: { code: 'internal_error', message: 'Something went wrong' },\n    });\n  };\n}\n\n/** JSON 404 for unknown /api routes. */\nexport function apiNotFoundHandler() {\n  return (req, res) => {\n    res.status(404).json({ error: { code: 'not_found', message: 'Not found' } });\n  };\n}"

A “branch protection rule” is a persistent merge restriction bound to one exact branch name, displayed in the frontend under Settings → Branches, with the backend storing the branch name and rule toggles. This product supports only two independently selectable requirements: “at least 1 valid Approve from someone other than the PR author” and “required check `test` is success”. For a given reviewer on the same current compare commit, only the reviewer's latest decision among Comment, Approve, and Request changes counts. The PR author cannot satisfy the approval requirement; any valid Request changes blocks merging; when the compare branch gains a new commit, all decisions become stale and a new review is required. “Checks” is the results area on the PR detail page attached to the current compare commit; the `test` status of pending, success, or failure, together with the setter and time, must be stored, and only a repository Admin may update that status from this area. The system blocks direct writes to a protected branch and applies the rule during subsequent PR merging. The repository provides a Settings link followed by a Branches link. The rule form opens from the Add branch protection rule button and contains a field labeled Branch name pattern (an exact branch name, with no wildcard semantics), checkboxes named Require 1 approval and Require status check test, and a Create button; an existing rule uses Save changes. After saving and reloading, the branch name is visible verbatim with the summaries 1 approval and Require status check test. For a non-Admin, Add branch protection rule is absent, even if Settings or Branches remain accessible; rejecting a save alone is insufficient. On the PR detail page, the Checks area is available on arrival, displays test: pending initially, and offers the Admin a combobox named test status with a clickable option named success and a Save button.           "content": "# M4a（Issue local/run#6）设计草案 v1 — 内容模型与代码浏览：文件/历史/diff\n\n权威需求：`input/requirements.yaml` REQ-4-1 / REQ-4-2-1 / REQ-4-2-2；参考图\n`input/reference/github-code-file-browser.png`（实为 overview 重复）、`github-commit-history.png`、`github-commit-diff.png`。\n共享依据：`docs/architecture.md` §5 内容模型、§7.1 仓库读路径契约（含 5 条补充约定）、§9.1/9.2；\n`docs/product-plan.md` §3；`docs/seed-data.md`；本 Issue comment #33/#35/#44/#84。\n\n## 0. 现状（origin/develop @ 53532a0）\n\n- M3 已发布 `GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/contents?branch=&path=` 与页面\n  `/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*`、`/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*`（只读最小版：分支徽标 + 面包屑 + 文件内容 + 目录直接子项 + 头 commit 的作者/消息）。\n- 表结构已就绪：`commits(id, repo_id, parent_id, author_id, message, created_at, tree_json)`、\n  `commit_files(commit_id, path, status, additions, deletions)`、`branches(head_commit_id)`、\n  `commit_parents`（§9.2 双父）。**不需要迁移**。\n- seed：`backend/src/seed/repos.js`，acme-docs = main（Initial commit → Document search flow）+ feature-search\n  （Add main-only notes）；文件 README.md / src/search.ts / docs/guide.md / main-only.md；`commit_files` 已写。\n- 待更正的登记近似：contents.commit = 分支头 commit（应为“最后触碰该 path 的提交”，约定 4）；空分支 404\n  （应为 200 空目录，约定 5）。\n- 文案契约 `frontend/src/contracts/copy.ts`；错误格式 `errors[]`；权限 `backend/src/permissions/index.js`。\n\n## 1. 任务范围\n\nREQ-4-1（文件/目录浏览）、REQ-4-2-1（提交历史，仓库/分支范围 + 文件范围）、REQ-4-2-2（commit/revision diff），\n外加约定 4/5 更正、树/commit/diff API + Vitest、seed 增量、Playwright 三条场景。\n\n## 2. 后端 API 契约（拟登记；M6a/#9 消费）\n\n统一前缀 `/api/repos/:owner/:name`，全部经 `loadRepo` + `requireViewable`：不可见仓库 / 未知 branch/path/rev → `404 not_found`（防枚举）。\n\n### 2.1 `GET /contents?branch=&path=`（既有，扩展）\n\n- `contents.commit` 更正为**最后触碰该 path 的提交**（沿 branch head 的第一父链向历史回溯，取最近一个让该 path 内容发生变化的 commit；目录语义 = 该前缀下任一文件变化）。返回 `{id, shortId, message, createdAt, author}`。\n- 存在但无提交的分支：无 path → `200 {type:'directory', branch, path:'', entries:[]}`；带 path → `404`（该 path 不存在）。\n- 其余字段/编码规则保持不变。\n\n### 2.2 `GET /commits?branch=<branch>&path=<path>&limit=<n>`\n\n- 默认 `branch` = 默认分支（`branch` 仍可显式给出）。\n- 顺序：`created_at DESC, id DESC`（seed 若同秒也必须倒序稳定）。\n- `path` 给出时只返回「相对第一父该 path 内容变化」的 commit。\n- 载荷：\n```json\n{ \"branch\": \"main\", \"path\": null, \"count\": 2,\n  \"commits\": [ { \"id\": 12, \"shortId\": \"000000c\", \"message\": \"Document search flow\",\n      \"author\": \"alice-dev\", \"createdAt\": \"2026-09-27T09:00:00.000Z\",\n      \"parentId\": 11, \"parentShortId\": \"000000b\",\n      \"changedFiles\": [ {\"path\":\"README.md\",\"status\":\"modified\",\"additions\":1,\"deletions\":1} ],\n      \"url\": \"/alice-dev/acme-docs/commit/000000c\" } ] }\n```\n\n### 2.3 `GET /commits/:rev`\n\n单条 commit 记录（形状同 `commits[]` 一项）+ `repository` 归属；`rev` 见 §2.6。\n\n### 2.4 `GET /commits/:rev/diff?base=<rev>&path=<path>`\n\n`base` 省略时 = 该 commit 的第一父（无父 → `base: null`，全部文件 `added`）。返回 §2.5 的 diff 载荷\n（`compare` = 该 commit）。`path` 给出时只返回该 path 的条目（文件 diff 页直开）。\n\n### 2.5 `GET /compare?base=<rev>&compare=<rev>&path=<path>`\n\n- 语义：**merge-base(base, compare) → compare** 的三点比较（GitHub 语义；commit diff 场景下 parent 即 merge base，两者一致）。\n- 载荷：\n```json\n{ \"base\":    { \"rev\":\"000000b\", \"id\":11, \"shortId\":\"000000b\", \"label\":\"main\", \"message\":\"Initial commit\", \"createdAt\":\"...\" },\n  \"compare\": { \"rev\":\"feature-search\", \"id\":13, \"shortId\":\"000000d\", \"label\":\"feature-search\", \"message\":\"Add main-only notes\", \"createdAt\":\"...\" },\n  \"mergeBase\": { \"id\":11, \"shortId\":\"000000b\" },\n  \"commits\": [ {\"id\":13,\"shortId\":\"000000d\",\"message\":\"...\",\"author\":\"alice-dev\",\"createdAt\":\"...\",\"url\":\"...\"} ],\n  \"files\": [ { \"path\":\"src/search.ts\", \"status\":\"modified\", \"additions\":1, \"deletions\":1,\n      \"url\":\"/alice-dev/acme-docs/commit/000000d/src/search.ts\",\n      \"lines\": [ {\"type\":\"context\",\"oldNumber\":1,\"newNumber\":1,\"text\":\"export function ...\"},\n                 {\"type\":\"deleted\",\"oldNumber\":2,\"newNumber\":null,\"text\":\"  return [];\"},\n                 {\"type\":\"added\",\"oldNumber\":null,\"newNumber\":2,\"text\":\"  return query ? [query] : [];\"} ] } ],\n  \"totals\": { \"files\": 2, \"additions\": 2, \"deletions\": 2 },\n  \"empty\": false }\n```\n- `commits[]` = 从 compare 可达、从「merge base」不可达的 commit（REQ-6-2-2 的 “comparable commits”/Commit summary）。\n- `lines[].oldNumber/newNumber` 供 M6b 行内评论锚定（§5 `review_comments.compare_commit_id + line`）。\n- 无差异 → `empty: true`、`files: []`、`totals` 全 0（REQ-6-2-2 “No changes”）。\n\n### 2.6 修订引用 `rev` 解析\n\n`:rev` / `base` / `compare` 接受：数字 commit id | 短 hash（见 §3.1）| 分支名。解析顺序：分支名精确匹配 →\n数字 id → 短 hash；都不匹配 → `404`。均为仓库内解析（跨仓库 commit id 视为不存在）。\n\n## 3. 关键语义决定（待 advisor 复核）\n\n1. **短 hash 的生成**（无迁移）：`shortId = commit.id.toString(16).padStart(7, '0')`，确定性、形如 git 短 hash、\n   跨重启稳定；API 同时给出数字 `id` 作为“stored commit identifier”。备选：新增 `sha` 列（需迁移 + seed 回填）。\n2. **diff 的事实来源**：行级 diff 由两侧 `tree_json` 快照计算（LCS 行 diff）；文件列表/status/增删数同样以\n   tree diff 为准，`commit_files` 作为持久化变更记录保留，并在 Vitest 中断言二者一致（快照一致性）；\n   seed 中 `Initial commit` 的增删数需由 2/0 更正为 3/0（与实际行数一致）。\n3. **历史排序**：`created_at DESC, id DESC`（seed 同秒时仍稳定）。\n4. **相对时间**：自实现 `relativeTime()`，始终含 \"ago\"；<60s 归并为 \"1 minute ago\"（对抗同秒 seed）。\n   同时给 seed commit 显式 `created_at`（约 3/2/1 天前，幂等，不覆盖已有行）。\n5. **文件/目录范围判定**：与第一父比较（merge commit 用第一父，§9.2 第二父只用于合并可达性）。\n6. **只读**：本模块全部端点不写库；e2e 断言 compare/diff 前后分支 head 不变。\n7. **“Commits” 链接与计数**：链接 accessible name 恰为 `Commits`（token 从 copy.ts 取），计数放在链接内\n   `aria-hidden=\"true\"` 的徽标中（可见 “Commits 2”，读屏只读 “Commits”）；每页仅此一个匹配链接。\n8. **diff 行的读屏区分**：每行渲染视觉隐藏前缀词 “Added”/“Deleted”/“Unchanged”（+ `+`/`-` 视觉前缀 aria-hidden），\n   行容器带 `data-type`；Playwright 以可见文本 + sr-only 计数双断言。\n\n## 4. 前端路由与页面\n\n| 路由 | 页面/职责 |\n|---|---|\n| `/:owner/:repo` | Code 根页（复用概览）：默认分支徽标、文件/目录链接、**上方 Commits 链接（带计数）** |\n| `/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*` | 目录页：分支、面包屑（可回父目录）、直接子项链接、Commits 链接 |\n| `/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*` | 文件页：文件名、完整内容（完整文本值）、**该 path 最近 commit**（作者/消息/相对时间）、Commits 链接（→ 文件范围历史，带计数） |\n| `/:owner/:repo/commits[/:branch]` | 历史页：`/commits` 重定向到 `/commits/<defaultBranch>`；倒序记录 = 短 hash 链接（accessible name 恰为短 hash）→ commit 详情、消息、作者、相对时间、该 commit 的 changed files |\n| `/:owner/:repo/commits/:branch/*` | 文件（或目录）范围历史：只含改动该 path 的 commit |\n| `/:owner/:repo/commit/:rev[/<path>]` | commit diff 页：base（parent）/compare 标识、`Changed files` 汇总、每文件增删数、行级 diff（新增/删除可读屏区分）、changed file 链接进入单文件 diff、parent 可见 |\n| `/:owner/:repo/compare?base=&compare=` | 比较页：原生 `select`（combobox）`base` / `compare` + `Compare` 按钮（命名与 REQ-6-2-2 对齐，M6a 复用同一 diff 组件与 API） |\n\n## 5. Seed 变更（幂等）\n\n- acme-docs 三个 commit 显式 `created_at`（约 3/2/1 天前），`ensureCommit` 增参、插入用 `ON CONFLICT`/存在即跳过。\n- `commit_files` 增删数与实际行 diff 对齐（Initial commit 各文件 3/0）。\n- 不再新增仓库/分支：`docs/guide.md`（仅 Initial commit 触碰）承担“文件范围历史排除未改动该文件的 commit”断言，\n  README.md（两个 commit 都触碰）承担正例。\n\n## 6. 检查方案\n\n- Vitest（`backend/test/content.test.js`）：contents 的约定 4/5；历史排序与范围过滤；行级 diff（root 全 added、新增/删除/修改、\n  无差异、行号）；compare merge-base 与 `commits[]`/`totals`；`commit_files` 与 tree diff 一致性；权限（私有仓库访客 404、\n  跨仓库 commit id 404）；只读（调用前后 branches/commits 行数不变）。\n- Playwright（`e2e/content.spec.ts`）：REQ-4-1（目录→文件→完整文本→面包屑返回→刷新保持）、REQ-4-2-1（仓库页 Commits →\n  倒序记录含短 hash/消息/作者/“ago” → 文件范围排除未改动 commit → 点 commit 进详情 parent/changed files 可见 → 刷新稳定）、\n  REQ-4-2-2（短 hash 直开 diff：base/compare 标识、`src/search.ts` 精确文本、`Changed files`、增删数、未变更文件不出现、\n  行级 diff 可读屏区分、刷新只读不改分支）。种子内容断言：README 完整文本值、相对时间含 \"ago\"、面包屑返回。\n\n## 7. 待决问题（请 advisor 给反例/替代）\n\nQ1 `rev` 接受分支名是否合理（M6a 需要 base/compare 分支比较，M4b 分支 API 属另一任务）？是否应改为只收 commit id +\n   M6a 自行解析分支头？\nQ2 compare 用 merge-base（三点）还是 base..compare（两点）更稳？M6a 的 “comparable commits”、“Create PR” 空差异判定、\n   M6b 行内评论锚点分别依赖哪一侧？\nQ3 短 hash 由 id 的 16 进制派生是否可接受（无迁移）？是否有更不易被验收脚本误判的形式？\nQ4 `commit_files` 与 tree diff 双源（一致性断言 + 计算为准）是否过度设计？能否只留一个事实来源？\nQ5 “Commits” 链接的 accessible name 恰好为 `Commits` 而计数 aria-hidden，与需求 “a commit count may accompany the\n   visible label” 及 WHEN “clicks the commit-count link” 是否有反例风险？\nQ6 `/commit/:rev` 与 `/compare` 两个页面是否必要（REQ-4-2-2 的 WHEN 是 “or”）？路由命名是否有更省事且不冲突的方案？\nQ7 文件范围历史用第一父比较是否有反例（需求只说 “parent commit”/“最新提交”）？\nQ8 需求原文里还有哪些未覆盖的验收点（尤其 4-2-2 的 “Changed files” 汇总、4-2-1 的“点击 commit 进详情后 parent 与\n   changed files 仍可见”）？\n"

Idempotent. */\nexport function migrate(db) {\n  db.exec(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (\n    version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name    TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    applied_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n  )`);\n  const applied = new Set(db.prepare('SELECT version FROM schema_migrations').all().map((r) => r.version));\n  for (const migration of migrations) {\n    if (applied.has(migration.version)) continue;\n    db.transaction(() => {\n      db.exec(migration.sql);\n      db.prepare('INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, name) VALUES (?, ?)').run(migration.version, migration.name);\n    })();\n  }\n  return db;\n}\n\n/** Open + migrate in one call (used by the server and by tests). */\nexport function initDb(dbPath) {\n  return migrate(openDb(dbPath));\n}\n\n/**\n * Run `fn` inside a single transaction and return its result.\n * better-sqlite3 transactions are synchronous and roll back on any throw.\n */\nexport function transact(db, fn) {\n  return db.transaction(fn)();\n}\n\n/** Uniform UTC timestamp used by manually written timestamps. */\nexport function nowIso() {\n  return new Date().toISOString().replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 19);\n}\n/**\n * Seed registry (docs/architecture.md §6).\n *\n * Each business module exports an idempotent `seedXxx(db)` function and\n * registers it here; `runSeeds()` executes the registry inside transactions at\n * server startup. Seeds are keyed by business identity and must be safe to run\n * repeatedly without overwriting data the user already produced (for example an\n * account whose password was changed through the UI must keep its new password\n * after a restart).\n */\nimport { transact } from '../db/index.js';\n\n/** @type {Map<string, (db: import('better-sqlite3').Database) => void>} */\nconst registry = new Map();\n\n/**\n * @param {string} name unique seed name, e.g. 'accounts'\n * @param {(db: import('better-sqlite3').Database) => void} fn idempotent seed body\n */\nexport function registerSeed(name, fn) {\n  if (registry.has(name)) {\n    throw new Error(`Seed \"${name}\" is already registered`);\n  }\n  registry.set(name, fn);\n}\n\nexport function registeredSeeds() {\n  return [...registry.keys()];\n}\n\n/**\n * Run every registered seed. A failure rolls back only the failing seed, so a\n * broken module seed cannot corrupt data written by the others.\n * @returns {string[]} names that ran\n */\nexport function runSeeds(db, { logger = console } = {}) {\n  const ran = [];\n  for (const [name, fn] of registry) {\n    try {\n      transact(db, () => fn(db));\n      ran.push(name);\n    } catch (error) {\n      logger.error(`[seed] \"${name}\" failed:`, error);\n      throw error;\n    }\n  }\n  return ran;\n}\n\n/** Test helper: clear the registry (only used between isolated test suites). */\nexport function resetSeedRegistry() {\n  registry.clear();\n}\n/**\n * Import every seed module exactly once, then run the registry.\n *\n * Business modules append one import line here (or import this file from their\n * own bootstrap) so their `registerSeed(...)` calls are executed.\n */\nimport './accounts.js';\nimport './repos.js';\n\nexport { registerSeed, runSeeds, registeredSeeds, resetSeedRegistry } from './index.js';\nimport { initDb } from '../src/db/index.js';\nimport '../src/seed/all.js';\nimport { runSeeds } from '../src/seed/index.js';\n\n/** Fresh in-memory database with migrations + seed data applied. */\nexport function createTestDb({ seed = true } = {}) {\n  const db = initDb(':memory:');\n  if (seed) runSeeds(db, { logger: { error: () => {} } });\n  return db;\n}\n\n/** Insert a user directly (test convenience). */\nexport function createUser(db, { username, email, passwordHash = 'x', verified = 1 }) {\n  return db\n    .prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO users (username, email, password_hash, email_verified)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) ORDER BY id').all(repo.id)) {\n  const tree = JSON.parse(c.tree_json);\n  const parent = c.parent_id ? '', tree[p] ?? '');\n    const cf = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM commit_files WHERE commit_id=? AND path=?').get(c.id, p);\n    console.log(`   ${p}: computed +${d.add} -${d.del} | commit_files`, cf ? `+${cf.additions} -${cf.deletions} (${cf.status})` : 'MISSING');\n  }\n}\nEOF\ncd /workspace/template/.factory26/20260929-042409-1202e245/braid-state/worktrees/issue-6/pi-deepseek-fast-g1/backend && /usr/local/bin/node /tmp/verify-seed.mjs 2>&1 | head -60"

not_found`）：\n\n- `GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/contents?branch=&path=`（§7.1 形状；M4a 完成约定 4/5 更正）\n- `GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/commits?branch=&path=&limit=` →\n  `{branch, path, count, commits:[{id, Q1–Q8 逐条\n\n### Q1（rev 接受分支名）— 结论：保留，但解析顺序有一个必须修的正确性 bug\n- 接受分支名合理：M6a REQ-6-2-2 的 base/compare 就是分支名（requirements.yaml:3074 \"selects `main` as base and `feature-search` as compare\"）；§7.1 \"diff API 以 commitId 为键\" 仍满足，因为载荷里带数字 `id`，M6a 以 id 落键（pull_requests.base_commit_id/compare_commit_id，architecture.md §5）。\n- **反例（必须修）**：登记的解析顺序「分支名 → 数字 id → 短 hash」（草案 §2.6，已写入工作区 architecture.md §7.2 约定 1）在短 hash 全为数字时解析到**错误的 commit**：id=16 的短 hash 是 `\"0000010\"`，按数字 id 先解析会得到 id=10。修复：形如 `/^[0-9a-f]{7}$/i` 的串一律按 16 进制短 hash 解析（它由 id 派生，hex→id 是无歧义双射），其余纯数字串按十进制 id。这比\"先数字后 hex\"更简单且无反例。\n- **反例 2（必须修）**：「仓库内解析（跨仓库 commit id 视为不存在）」对 **fork 是错的**。§9.1：fork 不复制 commit，fork 仓库分支指向 `repo_id` 属于源仓库的 commit（证据：backend/test/repos.test.js:296-304 断言 fork 的 commits 行数为 0 且分支 head 相同；e2e/repos.spec.ts REQ-3-2-2 断言 fork 里 README 链接可见）。若按 `commit.repo_id = 当前仓库` 过滤，fork 的历史/diff 全 404。应改为：**rev 相对\"本仓库分支可达的 commit 集\"解析**（沿分支 head 的第一父+§9.2 第二父可达性）。权限仍由仓库可见性门禁，无泄露面扩大。\n\n### Q2（三点 vs 两点）— 结论：三点（merge-base），草案正确，不要改\n- M6a \"comparable commits\"/Commit summary 消费 `commits[]` = compare 可达而 merge base 不可达（REQ-6-2-2 description \"calculates comparable commits\"，yaml:3051）→ 三点。\n- M6a 空差异判定（`empty:true` → \"No changes\" + 禁用 Create pull request，yaml:3052/3094）→ 三点。\n- M6b 行内评论锚点（§5 `review_comments.compare_commit_id + line`）依赖**三点 diff 的 newNumber**。反例：PR 打开后 base 分支前进并改了同一文件，两点 diff 会把 base 侧变更显示为删除行、newNumber 与 PR 实际展示的 diff 错位，锚点指向不存在的行。三点无此问题。\n- 注意写明：`/commits/:rev/diff?base=` 的显式 base 同样按 merge-base 语义（base 是祖先时与两点一致），避免 M6a 消费时语义分裂。\n\n### Q3（id 派生短 hash）— 结论：可接受，无迁移\n- 确定性、重启稳定、7 位 hex 形态像 git；\"references the stored commit identifier\"（REQ-4-2-1）由 shortId⇔id 双射满足。备选 sha 列需迁移+回填，零收益。\n- 唯一风险是 Q1 的解析歧义，按上面的修法消除。验收脚本取 hash 的入口是历史页/API 渲染值，不需要预知。\n\n### Q4（commit_files 与 tree diff 双源）— 结论：不算过度设计，但一致性断言的范围写法会假失败\n- 双源有必要：`commit_files` 是 §5 的持久化变更记录，M6 合并写路径要按 commit 查询；工作区 architecture.md §7.2 约定 3 已登记\"必须与 tree diff 一致（Vitest 断言）\"。\n- **反例（必须限定断言范围或改 seed 生成方式）**：全域一致性断言现在必然失败——\n  a. 测试中直插的 commit 无 commit_files 行：repos.test.js:118-130（'Add API client'）、:306-311（'Fork only change'，tree='{}' 还删除了 README）；\n  b. `seedSimpleRepository` 硬编码 additions:1（seed/repos.js:166），而 secret-research README 2 行、acme-docs-fork README 3 行、org-handbook README 2 行。\n- **建议形状**：seed 的 `ensureCommit` 不再硬编码增删数，改用与生产 diff 同一个行 diff 辅助函数由 parent tree vs tree 计算（幂等性不受影响，只在插入时填）。这样一致性断言可覆盖所有带 commit_files 行的 commit，且一并修好草案漏掉的 feature commit 错误（见 §3）。serve 侧仍以 tree diff 为准；文件范围历史可以用 commit_files.path（含目录前缀匹配）做成员判定，更便宜且语义等价。\n\n### Q5（Commits 链接形态）— 结论：草案形态正确，是最低风险解\n- 需求允许 \"a commit count may accompany the visible label without introducing another matching link\"（REQ-4-2-1 description）。accessible name 恰为 `Commits` 同时通过 exact 与 substring 匹配。\n- 反例（草案已规避，列出防回退）：a) name=\"Commits 2\" 会让 `getByRole('link',{name:'Commits',exact:true})` 落空；b) 计数做成独立链接且文本如 \"2 commits\" 会被 Playwright 的大小写不敏感 substring 匹配命中，造成 strict mode 双命中。aria-hidden 徽标两条都规避。注意 RepoNav 现有 Code/Issues/Pull requests（e2e/repos.spec.ts:262-264）不与 Commits 冲突；每页恰一个。\n- 补充：文件页的 Commits 链接指向文件范围历史，需求原文 \"The repository and file pages **each** expose one history link named 'Commits'\" 正合草案；但**计数语义要写明**（仓库页=分支 commit 数、文件页=该 path 的 commit 数），草案未写。\n\n### Q6（/commit/:rev 与 /compare 两页）— 结论：两页都要，但\"与 M6a 共用同一比较页\"的假设是错的，必须改\n- WHEN 的 \"or\" 意味着验收可走任一路：短 hash 直开（/commit/:rev）必须支持未导航直开（REQ-4-2-2 description \"without requiring previous navigation through history\"）；比较页在 WHEN 中被显式描述（\"on the comparison page and clicks 'Compare'\"），不能省。\n- **阻碍 M6a 的形状错误（必须改）**：草案 §4 假设 M6a 复用同一个 `/compare` 页面。REQ-6-2-2 实际要求：\n  1. 按钮名是 **\"Compare changes\"**（yaml:3054 \"Compare changes is a button\"），不是草案的 `Compare`；\n  2. 该比较页**仅 signed-in Write+ 可进**（yaml:3053 \"Only signed-in users with Write… may compare branches on the repository pull-request page\"），而 REQ-4-2-2 的比较页**访客必须用**（\"Any user with repository-view permission\"）；\n  3. REQ-6-2-2 的 select 选项是**分支名**（\"each option's visible label is its exact branch name\"），REQ-4-2-2 需要选 **parent commit / commit**（commit 值选项）。\n  - 应改成的形状：**两个页面、一套组件+API**。M4a 交付 `/compare`（全 viewer 可读、rev 选择器含 commit 选项）；M6a 交付 PR 创建比较流（\"New pull request\" 链接进入、Write+ 门禁、分支 select、\"Compare changes\"+\"Create pull request\"+\"No changes\"），复用同一 diff 组件与 `/api/.../compare` 端点。API 层无需分裂（权限在页面/PR 写路径门禁，读 diff 本身按仓库可见性）。这一点建议写进 architecture.md §7.2 的消费约定，避免 #9 直接继承错误假设。\n- 按钮令牌建议直接定为 **\"Compare changes\"**：REQ-4-2-2 的 \"clicks 'Compare'\" 用 substring 匹配可命中 \"Compare changes\"，REQ-6-2-2 的 exact 断言也满足；反之叫 \"Compare\" 会在 REQ-6-2-2 的 exact 断言下落空。\n- 路由无冲突：`/commit/:rev[/<path>]` 与 `/commits/:branch` 是不同静态段。\n\n### Q7（文件范围用第一父）— 结论：正确\n- 需求只说单数 \"parent commit\"；§9.2 定 parent_id=第一父。反例检验：M6 合并 commit 进入 main 后，被合并分支独有的文件变更会体现在 merge commit 的 tree 差里 → 文件范围历史把 merge commit 计为触碰者，符合直觉；分支侧中间 commit 不在 main 第一父链上不出现，属可记录的简化（验收种子是线性历史，不可观测）。无需改。\n\n### Q8（遗漏验收点）— 见下节，至少 6 条\n\n---\n\n## 2. 草案遗漏/有落空风险的验收点（对照原文）\n\n1. **REQ-4-1 THEN2 的分支切换无 UI 载体（最大缺口）**：\"After switching to another branch that does not contain the file, the file is not displayed\"。种子证人：`main-only.md` 仅存在于 feature-search（seed/repos.js:189）。现状：M3 文件页只有静态分支徽标（RepositoryContentsPage.tsx:62-66），全站无分支列表 API、无切换器；草案 §4 四张表都没有切换控件。仅靠改 URL 切换对自动验收是赌博。**必须补**：最小 `GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/branches`（只读：name+isDefault）+ blob/tree 页原生 `select`（combobox，建议名 \"Branch\"，选项=分支名原文）导航到同 path 的另一分支（缺失文件→404/\"not displayed\" 页面）。端点形状登记给 M4b 扩展而非另起。\n2. **\"distinct readable text values\"（REQ-4-2-1 description）**：消息与作者须各自可 exact 匹配、不截断、不与时间合并成一个 \"alice-dev committed 3 days ago\" 节点。现有文件页模式（author/message 分 span，RepositoryContentsPage.tsx:97-100）要保持到历史页。草案未写此约束。\n3. **changed-file 路径的 exact text（REQ-4-2-2 description）**：`src/search.ts` 必须落在单一文本节点（拆成 \"src/\"+\"search.ts\" 两个 span 会让 exact getByText 落空）。\n4. **增删数与 \"Changed files\" 汇总必须是可见文本**：\"+1 −1\"、\"Changed files\"、总数渲染为文本节点，不能只靠色条/颜色（\"numeric additions/deletions summary\"）。\n5. **sr-only diff 行的节点分离**：行文本（如 `return query ? [query] : [];`）须在独立节点内；sr-only 的 \"Added\"/\"Deleted\" 与 aria-hidden 的 `+`/`-` 各自成节点，否则行内容 exact/toContainText 断言被前缀污染。草案方向对，写明节点边界。\n6. **短 hash 链接唯一性**：历史页 `getByRole('link',{name:'000000c'})` 须 count=1；diff 页 base/compare 标识若也用短 hash 做**链接**，跨页无冲突，但同页内 base=compare 或标识与\"点这里看 parent\"链接同名时会双命中——标识用文本、parent 用链接即可。\n7. **REQ-4-2-2 权限 THEN 的显式断言**：\"if the user lacks permission to view either revision, its diff content is not displayed\"——草案有 404 设计，但 §6 检查方案只写了 Vitest 私有仓库 404；补一条\"私有仓库 commit 的短 hash 直开 diff 页无内容泄露\"的 e2e/Vitest 断言。\n8. **REQ-4-2-2 GIVEN 的 'Add main-only notes' 入口**：该 commit 是\"含 added+modified 文件\"的种子 commit（added main-only.md + modified README），在 feature-search 头上；其 commit entry 须可经 `/commits/feature-search` 到达——草案路由支持，检查方案里点名补一条用例。\n9. **\"ago\" 的抗水合**：相对时间必须在首次渲染即为文本（含 \"ago\"），不能是客户端 hydration 后才填充的自定义元素；<60s 归并 \"1 minute ago\" 可接受。\n10. **compare 页 select 的选项集**：REQ-4-2-2 要求能选 parent commit 与 commit——select 选项须含 commit 值（短 hash 标签）；这与 REQ-6-2-2 的\"选项=分支名\"不同，再次支持两页分离（Q6）。\n\n---\n\n## 3. Seed 改动对 M3/M6 既有断言的影响（已核实）\n\n- **显式 created_at：安全。** 无任何测试断言种子 commit 的时间值（grep backend/test 无 additions/created_at 相关断言）；`repoUpdatedAt`（service.js:116-119）只用于 e2e 的 `/^Updated /` 前缀断言（repos.spec.ts:67）。旧库幂等跳过已有行（seed/repos.js:79-81），排序由 `created_at DESC, id DESC` 的 id 兜底，平台新库拿到显式时间。注意 created_at 默认是 `datetime('now')` 格式（migrations.js:106），显式值的格式与 API 序列化/relativeTime 输入要一致。\n- **commit_files 2→3 更正：对 M3 安全，但草案漏了另外三处错误。** 现有唯一相关断言是新建仓库 commit_files 行数=1（repos.test.js:251），不受影响。草案只提了 Initial commit（实际每文件 3 行 ✓），漏了：\n  1. 'Add main-only notes' 的 README：searching 版追加 `\"\\nThe branch also keeps notes.\\n\"` = **+2/−0**（现记 1/0，seed/repos.js:199）；\n  2. main-only.md 实际 3 行 = **+3/−0**（现记 2/0）；\n  3. `seedSimpleRepository` 系仓库的 README 增删数（§Q4-b）。\n  按 Q4 建议改为 seed 内计算，四处一起消失。\n- **对 M6 的影响与协商点**：更正后 feature-search→main 的 tree diff = main-only.md(+3) + README(+2/−0)，合计 5/0、2 文件、**不含 src/search.ts**。而 docs/seed-data.md:71 记载 M6 计划的 PR 'Improve onboarding' 是 \"1 添加+1 修改、`src/search.ts`、3 additions 1 deletions\"，REQ-6-2-2 GIVEN 也说 \"feature-search … modifies one file\"、变更文件为 `src/search.ts`（yaml:3073/3076）——**当前树与本就矛盾**（src/search.ts 在两分支相同），说明 M6 必然要自带新 commit 来塑形，M4a 的更正当不了它的数字，也不应去凑。**反向风险**：若 M6 为凑数字改动 feature-search 的既有 commit/树而删掉 main-only.md，会拆掉 REQ-4-1 的\"分支不含该文件\"证人。需在根 Issue 或 #9 留言钉住：main-only.md 证人是 REQ-4-1 资产，M6 塑形用新分支/新 commit。\n- **guide.md 承担反例：无 seed 结构变化，安全。** 它自 M3 存在且被断言（repos.test.js:136-139）。它是文件范围历史唯一的排除证人（README.md 在三个 commit 全被触碰，main 上文件范围=分支范围，无法观测排除）——草案判断正确，检查方案须用 `docs/guide.md` 断言\"不含 Document search flow\"。\n\n---\n\n## 4. 必须修改项清单（按优先级）\n\n1. **rev 解析**：7 位 hex 串优先按 16 进制短 hash 解析（修 id≥16 全数字 hash `repoContents(db, repo, 'main', 'docs/guide.md').commit` = **id 2（分支头）**，而最后触碰该文件的提交是 id 1\n      → 约定 4 缺陷可观测。\n    - 存在但无提交的分支：`repoContents(..., 'empty-branch', '')` = **`null`** → 约定 5 缺陷。\n    - `commit_files` 与两侧 tree 的行 diff 不一致：`Initial commit` 三文件各记 2/0（实际 3/0）；\n      `Add main-only notes` README 记 1/0（实际 2/0）、main-only.md 记 2/0（实际 3/0）；\n      `seedSimpleRepository` 各仓库硬编码 1/0。\n  - 需求侧事实：REQ-4-1 THEN2 要求“切换到不含该文件的分支后该文件不显示”，而 M3 文件页只有静态分支徽标、全站无分支列表\n    → M4a 必须补最小只读切换能力，否则该 THEN 无法被自动验收。\n  - 原生 advisor 独立复核：采纳其 6 项必须修改（rev 解析、fork 可达性、比较页拆分、分支切换缺口、seed 计数生成方式、\n    渲染/文案节点约束），见下“决定”。\n\n## Plan（实施负责人在独立工作区承接）\n\n1. 预演：`PATH=/usr/local/bin:$PATH pnpm install`；基线 `pnpm test`；必要时 `pnpm e2e` 确认基线绿（M3 packet 记录 53532a0\n   时 Vitest 63/63、Playwright 21/21）。\n2. 后端：`modules/content/service.js`（行级 diff、merge base、rev 解析、范围历史、最近触碰 path 的提交）、\n   `modules/content/router.js`（commits/commit/diff/compare/branches）挂到同一 `/api/repos` 前缀；\n   先让 `backend/test/content.test.js` 绿，再补 `contents` 约定 4/5 更正。\n3. 前端：`RepoCommitsPage`（分支/文件范围）、`CommitDiffPage`、`CompareRevisionsPage` + 共享 diff 组件；\n   Code 页/目录页/文件页各一个 `Commits` 链接（计数 `aria-hidden` 徽标）、文件页“最近 commit”改为该 path 的提交、\n   `Branch` combobox 切换；`copy.ts` 增补（`Commits`、`Changed files`、`Compare changes`、`base`/`compare`、\n   `Added`/`Deleted`/`Unchanged`、`Branch`）；路由按 §7.2。\n4. Seed：`ensureCommit` 用行 diff 助手计算增删数；显式递进 `created_at`；幂等、不覆盖用户数据。\n5. 检查：`pnpm test`（含全量一致性断言）、`pnpm e2e`（`e2e/content.spec.ts` + 既有 spec）、\n   `bash checks/platform-path.sh`；把命令、提交、结果写进 PR 评论并回填本 packet。\n\n## 决定（2026-09-29，含 advisor 复核结论）\n\n1. **API 形状 = `docs/architecture.md` §7.2 + Issue #6 正文登记**：`commits` / `commits/:rev` / `commits/:rev/diff` /\n   `compare` / `branches`；以 commitId 为键（载荷带数字 `id` 与 7 位 hex 短 hash，M6a 以 id 落\n   `pull_requests.base_commit_id/compare_commit_id`）；`lines[].oldNumber/newNumber` 供 M6b 行内评论锚定。\n2. **rev 解析（advisor 修正）**：`/^[0-9a-f]{7}$/i` 一律按 16 进制短 hash（否则 id≥16 的 `0000010` 会被当十进制 10）；\n   其后纯数字按十进制 id，再按分支名；候选不存在则顺次下移，全部不命中 → 404。\n3. **解析域 = 本仓库分支可达 commit 集（advisor 修正）**：§9.1 fork 只复制分支指针，`commit.repo_id` 属源仓库；\n   若按 repo_id 过滤会让 fork 的历史/diff 全部 404。\n4. **compare = merge-base → compare（三点）**：M6a 的 comparable commits / 空差异判定、M6b 的锚点都依赖三点语义；\n   `/commits/:rev/diff?base=` 的显式 base 同样按 merge-base 语义（base 为祖先时与两点一致）。\n5. **比较页拆分（advisor 修正，写入 §7.2 与 §9.14）**：M4a 交付全 viewer 可读的 revision 比较页（选项为 commit，\n   按钮令牌 `Compare changes`——同时满足 REQ-4-2-2 的 substring “Compare” 与 REQ-6-2-2 的 exact）；\n   M6a 自建 Write+ 门禁的 PR 比较流（分支选项、“New pull request”/“Create pull request”/“No changes”），\n   共享 diff 组件与 `/api/.../compare`。\n6. **最小只读分支切换归 M4a**：`GET /api/repos/:owner/:name/branches` + blob/tree/Code 页 combobox `Branch`\n   （选项=精确分支名），满足 REQ-4-1 THEN2；REQ-4-3-1 的 `Branch <name>` 按钮 + `Find branch` 归 M4b（#7）扩展/替换。\n   已在本 Issue 请根负责人确认归属（按验收必需推断，不阻塞实施）。\n7. **seed 增删数由行 diff 助手在插入时计算**（advisor 修正）：一致性 Vitest 覆盖全部带 `commit_files` 的 commit；\n   同时修正 feature commit 与 simple 仓库的硬编码值；不新增仓库/分支。\n8. **`Commits` 链接形态**：accessible name 恰为 `Commits`，计数放在链接内 `aria-hidden` 徽标（可见 “Commits 2”，\n   读屏只读 “Commits”）；每页恰一个匹配链接；计数语义 = 该范围 commit 数（分支 / 该 path）。\n9. **渲染/文案节点约束（advisor 修正，写入 §7.2 约定 7）**：消息/作者/相对时间/文件路径各自独立文本节点；\n   增删数为可见文本；历史页短 hash 是唯一链接、diff 页 base/compare 标识用文本；diff 行内代码文本、`aria-hidden`\n   的 `+`/`-`、视觉隐藏的 `Added`/`Deleted`/`Unchanged` 各自独立成节点。\n10. **相对时间自实现**：始终含 \"ago\"，<60s 归并 `1 minute ago`（对抗同秒 seed）；不在客户端 hydration 后才填充。\n11. **文件/目录范围用第一父比较**（merge commit 用第一父；验证种子为线性历史）。\n12. **只读**：本模块端点不写库；e2e 断言 compare/diff 前后分支 head 不变。\n13. **seed 证人钉住（通知 M6/#9）**：`main-only.md` 是 REQ-4-1 THEN2 的唯一种子证人，M6 的 PR 塑形不得移除它；\n    feature-search → main 更正后的实际 diff = 共享决定（必须遵循）\ndocs/architecture.md §5 内容模型（commit 共享不可变、branch=指针、tree_json 快照、commit_files）、**§7.2 代码浏览/历史/版本比较契约（M4a 登记）**、§9.1/9.2/§9.14；docs/product-plan.md §3；docs/seed-data.md（acme-docs 内容种子）。\n本模块交付的 diff/树 API 是 M6a 比较页与 Files changed 的直接依赖：API 形状（base/compare → 变更文件列表 + 每文件 additions/deletions + 行级 diff）一经发布在 Issue 评论登记为准。\n\n## 共享契约（M4a 登记，权威入口；#7/#9/#10 消费）\n\n页面路由（在 §7.1 之上扩展，不新建第二套）：\n- `/:owner/:repo`、`/:owner/:repo/tree/:branch/*`：Code 页/目录页；页面上唯一历史链接 accessible name 恰为 `Commits`（计数为 `aria-hidden` 徽标，可见 “Commits 2”）；另提供最小只读 `Branch` combobox 切换分支（REQ-4-1 THEN2 需要）。\n- `/:owner/:repo/blob/:branch/*`：文件页；`Commits` 指向该 path 的范围历史；“最近 commit” = 最后触碰该 path 的提交。\n- `/:owner/:repo/commits[/:branch][/*path]`：分支/文件范围历史，`created_at DESC, id DESC`；`/:owner/:repo/commit/:rev[/<path>]`：commit diff 页（base = 第一父）。\n- `/:owner/:repo/compare?base=&compare=`：revision 比较页（全 viewer 可读；combobox `base`/`compare`，选项为 commit 短 hash；按钮 `Compare changes`）。REQ-6-2-2 的 PR 比较流由 M6a 自建页面（Write+、分支选项），复用同一 diff 组件与 `/api/.../compare`。\n\n数据端点（`/api/repos/:owner/:name` 前缀；全部经 `loadRepo` + `requireViewable`；不可见仓库 / 未知 branch、path、rev → `404 not_found`；只读）：\n- `GET /contents?branch=&path=`（§7.1 形状；M4a 更正约定 4/5：`commit` = 最后触碰该 path 的提交；空分支 → 200 空目录）\n- `GET /branches` → `{branches:[{name,isDefault,headCommitId,shortId}]}`\n- `GET /commits?branch=&path=&limit=` → `{branch,path,count,commits:[{id,shortId,message,author,createdAt,parentId,parentShortId,changedFiles:[{path,status,additions,deletions}],url}]}`\n- `GET /commits/:rev` → `{commit:{…}}`\n- `GET /commits/:rev/diff?base=&path=` → diff 载荷\n- `GET /compare?base=&compare=&path=` → diff 载荷：\n  `{base:{rev,id,shortId,label,message,createdAt}|null, compare:{…}, mergeBase:{id,shortId}|null, commits:[{id,shortId,message,author,createdAt,url}], files:[{path,status,additions,deletions,url,lines:[{type,oldNumber,newNumber,text}]}], totals:{files,additions,deletions}, empty}`\n\n约定：\n1. `rev`/`base`/`compare` 接受 7 位 hex 短 hash（`id` 的 16 进制左补零；形如 `/^[0-9a-f]{7}$/i` 优先按 hex 解析）→ 十进制 id → 分支名；解析域 = **本仓库分支可达 commit 集**（fork 直接读共享不可变 commit，§9.1）。短 hash 由 id 派生，不新增 `sha` 列。\n2. `compare` = **merge-base(base,compare) → compare**（三点）；`commits[]` = compare 可达、merge base 不可达（M6a 的 comparable commits / 空差异判定），`lines[].oldNumber/newNumber` 供 M6b 行内评论锚定。\n3. `commit_files` 与 tree diff 必须一致（seed 用同一行 diff 助手计算 + 全量 Vitest 断言）；服务端以 tree diff 为事实来源。\n4. 渲染/文案约束（影响自动验收）：消息/作者/相对时间/文件路径各自独立文本节点；增删数为可见文本；历史页短 hash 为唯一链接、diff 页 base/compare 用文本；diff 行的代码文本、`aria-hidden` 的 `+`/`-`、视觉隐藏的 `Added`/`Deleted`/`Unchanged` 各自成节点。\n5. 跨任务边界：最小只读分支端点 + `Branch` combobox 归 M4a，#7 扩展/替换为 REQ-4-3-1 的完整选择器；`main-only.md` 是 REQ-4-1 THEN2 的唯一种子证人，#9 的 PR 塑形不得移除它。\n\n## 交付\n1. 文件浏览：Code 页目录列表（目录/文件条目为链接，accessible name = 各自名称）、路径面包屑（可返回父目录）、当前分支与路径；文件页显示完整存储内容（完整文本值可见）、最近 commit、文件名；刷新保持分支/路径/内容。\n2. 提交历史：仓库与文件页各一个历史链接 \"Commits\"（可带计数）；历史页按时间倒序，含 short hash、消息、作者、相对时间（含 \"ago\"）；文件范围历史只显示改动该文件的 commit；点击 commit 进详情（parent、changed files 保持可见）。\n3. commit diff：diff 页显示 base/compare 标识、变更文件列表、每文件增删行；新增/删除行可被读屏区分；\"Changed files\" 汇总与数值统计；未变更文件不出现；只读不产生写操作。\n4. 后端：树/commit/diff/compare/branches API + Vitest（快照一致性、diff 计算、权限：无 view 权限不返回内容）。\n5. seed：acme-docs 的 README.md/src/search.ts/docs/、main+feature-search 分支、\"Document search flow\" commit（幂等）；commit 显式递进 `created_at`；`commit_files` 增删数由行 diff 计算。\n6. 最小只读分支切换（`GET /branches` + `Branch` combobox）：满足 REQ-4-1 THEN2 的验收必需项，完整分支能力归 #7。\n\n## 前置\nM3（仓库概览/创建）已合入 develop（仓库对象与权限判定可用）。\n\n## 验收\nPlaywright 覆盖 3 条需求场景；种子内容断言（完整文本值、\"ago\"
